# Raw msprof `op_summary` evidence — GDR fwd+bwd concurrency rounds, 2026-10-14

These are the unmodified `op_summary` CSVs msprof produced for the runs quoted in
`../PERF_REPORT.md`, pasted verbatim as fenced blocks.

**Why pasted rather than committed as `.csv`:** this repo's `.gitignore` excludes
`*.csv` repo-wide (line 27), and AGENTS.md §10 forbids committing profiling traces.
These are per-op *summaries*, not traces, and sibling units under
`kernels/projects/a5/` commit their evidence as `.json`/`.log`. Nothing here was
edited, filtered or reformatted — copy a block back out to recover the original file.

Passes: `base` is the 1048.3 µs starting point, `opt1..3` are round 1 (978.0 µs),
`round2_opt1..3` are round 2 (912.4 µs). `PipeUtilization` is the group the per-kernel
table derives from; the other six groups were captured once, on the baseline.

Measured on a 28 cube / 56 vector Ascend950PR (DAV_3510, CANN 9.1.0-beta.1-950) at
B=1, T=2048, H=8, K=V=128. Device identity omitted per AGENTS.md §5.

---

# Large-shape run — B=1, T=16384, H=16, K=V=128

Added 2026-10-14, after the concurrency rounds above, on the same part and
toolchain. Two independent `PipeUtilization` passes, warmup 2 / repeats 3, warm
medians with the first row per kernel dropped as JIT-contaminated. Totals
14246.9 and 14238.5 us. This shape is 16x the work of the B=1/T=2048/H=8
reference (8x T, 2x H) and is **timing-only** — the CPU oracle was not run at this
size, so correctness is asserted only at the five composition cases up to T=2048.
